{"forms":[{"form":"tricksters","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["Any of numerous figures featuring in various mythologies and folk traditions, who use guile and secret knowledge to challenge authority and play tricks and pranks on others with their acts of trickery; any similar figure in literature."],"topics":["human-sciences","literature","media","mysticism","mythology","philosophy","publishing","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"A skilled trickster knows how to outsmart their enemies with ease.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["One who plays tricks or pranks on others."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Our fellow trickster will perform some impressive magic tricks tonight.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["One who performs tricks (parts of a magician act or entertainingly difficult physical actions)."]},{"glosses":["An impish or playful person."]},{"examples":[{"text":"That shady trickster faked being a psychic advisor to spread misinformation and gain profit.","type":"example"},{"text":"She's easy game for a trickster like him.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A fraud or cheat (person who performs a trick or hoax full of falsehoods for the purpose of unlawful gain)."]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈtɹɪk.stə/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"[ˈtɹ̝̊ʷɪk.stə]","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈtɹɪk.stɚ/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"[ˈtɹ̝̊ɪk.stɚ] ~ [ˈtɹ̝̊ɪk.stɹ̩]","tags":["General-American"]}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"one who plays tricks or pranks on others","word":"prankster"},{"sense":"one who plays tricks or pranks on others","word":"tricker"},{"sense":"one who plays tricks or pranks on others","word":"trepan"},{"sense":"one who performs tricks","word":"tricker"}],"upstream_line":221998,"upstream_offset":1076899134,"upstream_record_sha256":"890ede1643441eb86c801a033658438fcacc2cc9e5582abe69188a1823c45bff","word":"trickster"}
